    \ExtensionTok{log} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{BLUE\}}\StringTok{[}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\%Y{-}\%m{-}\%d \%H:\%M:\%S\textquotesingle{}}\VariableTok{)}\StringTok{]}\VariableTok{$\{NC\}}\StringTok{ }\VariableTok{$\{RED\}}\StringTok{[ERROR]}\VariableTok{$\{NC\}}\StringTok{ }\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar dependencias}
\FunctionTok{check\_dependencies()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Verificando dependencias..."}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \OtherTok{! }\BuiltInTok{command} \AttributeTok{{-}v}\NormalTok{ stow }\OperatorTok{\&\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ /dev/null}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"Stow no está instalado"}
        \BuiltInTok{read} \AttributeTok{{-}p} \StringTok{"¿Instalar stow? [Y/n] "} \AttributeTok{{-}n}\NormalTok{ 1 }\AttributeTok{{-}r}
        \BuiltInTok{echo}
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \KeywordTok{[[} \OtherTok{!} \VariableTok{$REPLY} \OtherTok{=\textasciitilde{}} \PreprocessorTok{\^{}}\OperatorTok{[}\SpecialStringTok{Nn}\OperatorTok{]$} \KeywordTok{]];} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{command} \AttributeTok{{-}v}\NormalTok{ apt }\OperatorTok{\&\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ /dev/null}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
                \FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt update }\KeywordTok{\&\&} \FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt install }\AttributeTok{{-}y}\NormalTok{ stow}
            \ControlFlowTok{elif} \BuiltInTok{command} \AttributeTok{{-}v}\NormalTok{ pacman }\OperatorTok{\&\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ /dev/null}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
                \FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ stow}
            \ControlFlowTok{elif} \BuiltInTok{command} \AttributeTok{{-}v}\NormalTok{ brew }\OperatorTok{\&\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ /dev/null}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
                \ExtensionTok{brew}\NormalTok{ install stow}
            \ControlFlowTok{else}
                \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"No se pudo instalar stow automáticamente"}
                \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}
            \ControlFlowTok{fi}
        \ControlFlowTok{else}
            \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"✓ Dependencias OK"}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Backup de archivo/directorio existente}
\FunctionTok{backup\_if\_exists()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{path}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{name}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$2}\StringTok{"}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}e} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$path}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]} \KeywordTok{\&\&} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{!} \OtherTok{{-}L} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$path}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_warn} \StringTok{"Existe: }\VariableTok{$path}\StringTok{"}
        \FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"}
        \FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$path}\StringTok{"} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Backup: }\VariableTok{$name}\StringTok{ → }\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"}
        \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{0}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{1}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar conflictos antes de stow}
\FunctionTok{check\_conflicts()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}\&}\DecValTok{1} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \AttributeTok{{-}q} \StringTok{"WARNING\textbackslash{}|ERROR"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{1}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{0}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Stow paquete con manejo de errores}
\FunctionTok{stow\_package()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{force}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{2}\OperatorTok{:{-}}\NormalTok{false}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{!} \OtherTok{{-}d} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"Paquete no existe: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{1}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Procesando: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Check conflicts}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \OtherTok{! }\ExtensionTok{check\_conflicts} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_warn} \StringTok{"Conflictos detectados en: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$force}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"true"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Forzando instalación..."}
            \CommentTok{\# Aquí podrías implementar lógica de backup automático}
        \ControlFlowTok{else}
            \BuiltInTok{read} \AttributeTok{{-}p} \StringTok{"¿Continuar? [y/N] "} \AttributeTok{{-}n}\NormalTok{ 1 }\AttributeTok{{-}r}
            \BuiltInTok{echo}
            \ControlFlowTok{if} \KeywordTok{[[} \OtherTok{!} \VariableTok{$REPLY} \OtherTok{=\textasciitilde{}} \PreprocessorTok{\^{}}\OperatorTok{[}\SpecialStringTok{Yy}\OperatorTok{]$} \KeywordTok{]];} \ControlFlowTok{then}
                \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"Saltado: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
                \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{1}
            \ControlFlowTok{fi}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Stow}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}v} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"✓ Instalado: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{0}
    \ControlFlowTok{else}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"✗ Error al instalar: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{1}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Mostrar ayuda}
\FunctionTok{show\_help()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} EOF}
\StringTok{Uso: }\VariableTok{$0}\StringTok{ [opciones] [paquetes...]}

\StringTok{Opciones:}
\StringTok{  {-}h, {-}{-}help          Mostrar esta ayuda}
\StringTok{  {-}a, {-}{-}all           Instalar todos los paquetes}
\StringTok{  {-}f, {-}{-}force         Forzar instalación (saltear prompts)}
\StringTok{  {-}l, {-}{-}list          Listar paquetes disponibles}
\StringTok{  {-}d, {-}{-}dry{-}run       Simular sin hacer cambios}

\StringTok{Ejemplos:}
\StringTok{  }\VariableTok{$0}\StringTok{ nvim zsh git     \# Instalar paquetes específicos}
\StringTok{  }\VariableTok{$0}\StringTok{ {-}{-}all            \# Instalar todo}
\StringTok{  }\VariableTok{$0}\StringTok{ {-}{-}list           \# Ver paquetes disponibles}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Listar paquetes disponibles}
\FunctionTok{list\_packages()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Paquetes disponibles:"}
    \BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"}
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
        \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$\{package}\OperatorTok{\%}\NormalTok{/}\VariableTok{\}}
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{!=} \StringTok{".git"} \BuiltInTok{]} \KeywordTok{\&\&} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}d} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"  {-} }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{done}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Main}
\FunctionTok{main()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{force}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{false}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{dry\_run}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{false}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{packages}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{()}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Parse argumentos}
    \ControlFlowTok{while} \KeywordTok{[[} \VariableTok{$\#} \OtherTok{{-}gt}\NormalTok{ 0 }\KeywordTok{]];} \ControlFlowTok{do}
        \ControlFlowTok{case} \VariableTok{$1} \KeywordTok{in}
            \SpecialStringTok{{-}h}\KeywordTok{|}\SpecialStringTok{{-}{-}help}\KeywordTok{)}
                \ExtensionTok{show\_help}
                \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 0}
                \ControlFlowTok{;;}
            \SpecialStringTok{{-}a}\KeywordTok{|}\SpecialStringTok{{-}{-}all}\KeywordTok{)}
                \BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"}
                \VariableTok{packages}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{($(}\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}d} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{sed} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}s\#/\#\textquotesingle{}} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \AttributeTok{{-}v} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\^{}\textbackslash{}.\textquotesingle{}}\VariableTok{))}
                \BuiltInTok{shift}
                \ControlFlowTok{;;}
            \SpecialStringTok{{-}f}\KeywordTok{|}\SpecialStringTok{{-}{-}force}\KeywordTok{)}
                \VariableTok{force}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{true}
                \BuiltInTok{shift}
                \ControlFlowTok{;;}
            \SpecialStringTok{{-}l}\KeywordTok{|}\SpecialStringTok{{-}{-}list}\KeywordTok{)}
                \ExtensionTok{list\_packages}
                \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 0}
                \ControlFlowTok{;;}
            \SpecialStringTok{{-}d}\KeywordTok{|}\SpecialStringTok{{-}{-}dry{-}run}\KeywordTok{)}
                \VariableTok{dry\_run}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{true}
                \BuiltInTok{shift}
                \ControlFlowTok{;;}
            \PreprocessorTok{*}\KeywordTok{)}
                \VariableTok{packages}\OperatorTok{+=}\VariableTok{(}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{)}
                \BuiltInTok{shift}
                \ControlFlowTok{;;}
        \ControlFlowTok{esac}
    \ControlFlowTok{done}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Verificar que hay paquetes para instalar}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \VariableTok{$\{}\OperatorTok{\#}\VariableTok{packages}\OperatorTok{[@]}\VariableTok{\}} \OtherTok{{-}eq}\NormalTok{ 0 }\BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"No se especificaron paquetes"}
        \ExtensionTok{show\_help}
        \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Iniciar log}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"=== Instalación de Dotfiles ==="}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Directorio: }\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Paquetes: }\VariableTok{$\{packages}\OperatorTok{[*]}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Verificar dependencias}
    \ExtensionTok{check\_dependencies}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Cambiar a dotfiles directory}
    \BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"} \KeywordTok{||} \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Dry run si se especificó}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$dry\_run}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ true }\BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"=== DRY RUN ==="}
        \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{packages}\OperatorTok{[@]}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
            \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Simulating: }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
            \ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}
        \ControlFlowTok{done}
        \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 0}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Instalar paquetes}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{success}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{0}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{failed}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{0}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{packages}\OperatorTok{[@]}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \ExtensionTok{stow\_package} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$force}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \KeywordTok{((}\VariableTok{success}\OperatorTok{++}\KeywordTok{))}
        \ControlFlowTok{else}
            \KeywordTok{((}\VariableTok{failed}\OperatorTok{++}\KeywordTok{))}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{done}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Resumen}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{""}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"=== Resumen ==="}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Exitosos: }\VariableTok{$success}\StringTok{"}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \VariableTok{$failed} \OtherTok{{-}gt}\NormalTok{ 0 }\BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_warn} \StringTok{"Fallidos: }\VariableTok{$failed}\StringTok{"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}d} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Backups en: }\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Log completo en: }\VariableTok{$LOG\_FILE}\StringTok{"}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Ejecutar}
\ExtensionTok{main} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$@}\StringTok{"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Script 2: update.sh}\label{script-2-update.sh}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/update.sh}

\BuiltInTok{set} \AttributeTok{{-}e}

\VariableTok{DOTFILES}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles"}

\BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"🔄 Actualizando dotfiles..."}

\CommentTok{\# Pull latest changes}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}

\CommentTok{\# Restow todos los paquetes instalados}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$\{package}\OperatorTok{\%}\NormalTok{/}\VariableTok{\}}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Verificar si está stowed}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \FunctionTok{find} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{"} \AttributeTok{{-}maxdepth}\NormalTok{ 2 }\AttributeTok{{-}type}\NormalTok{ l }\AttributeTok{{-}lname} \StringTok{"*}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{/}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{/*"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \AttributeTok{{-}q}\NormalTok{ .}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"↻ Restowing }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{..."}
        \ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
\ControlFlowTok{done}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✓ Actualización completa!"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Script 3: check.sh}\label{script-3-check.sh}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/check.sh}

\VariableTok{DOTFILES}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles"}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"📋 Estado de paquetes:"}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"===================="}

\BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$\{package}\OperatorTok{\%}\NormalTok{/}\VariableTok{\}}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{".git"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ControlFlowTok{continue}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Buscar primer archivo del paquete}
    \VariableTok{first\_file}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{find} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \AttributeTok{{-}type}\NormalTok{ f }\AttributeTok{{-}o} \AttributeTok{{-}type}\NormalTok{ l }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{head} \AttributeTok{{-}1}\VariableTok{)}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}z} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$first\_file}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"⚠️  }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ (vacío)"}
        \ControlFlowTok{continue}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Convertir a path en HOME}
    \VariableTok{home\_path}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/}\VariableTok{$\{first\_file}\OperatorTok{\#}\VariableTok{$package}\NormalTok{/}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}L} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$home\_path}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \VariableTok{target}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{readlink} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$home\_path}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{)}
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \KeywordTok{[[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$target}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{==} \PreprocessorTok{*}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{/}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}\PreprocessorTok{*} \KeywordTok{]];} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{else}
            \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"⚠️  }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ (symlink apunta a: }\VariableTok{$target}\StringTok{)"}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{elif} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}e} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$home\_path}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"❌ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ (existe pero no es symlink)"}
    \ControlFlowTok{else}
        \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"❌ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ (no instalado)"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
\ControlFlowTok{done}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar symlinks rotos}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{""}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"🔗 Verificando symlinks rotos..."}
\VariableTok{broken\_links}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{find} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{"} \AttributeTok{{-}maxdepth}\NormalTok{ 3 }\AttributeTok{{-}xtype}\NormalTok{ l }\AttributeTok{{-}lname} \StringTok{"*}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{/*"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null}\VariableTok{)}

\ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}z} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$broken\_links}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ No hay symlinks rotos"}
\ControlFlowTok{else}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"⚠️  Symlinks rotos encontrados:"}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$broken\_links}\StringTok{"}
\ControlFlowTok{fi}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Script 4: clean.sh}\label{script-4-clean.sh}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/clean.sh}

\VariableTok{DOTFILES}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles"}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"🧹 Limpiando symlinks huérfanos..."}

\CommentTok{\# Encontrar symlinks rotos que apuntan a dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{find} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{"} \AttributeTok{{-}maxdepth}\NormalTok{ 3 }\AttributeTok{{-}xtype}\NormalTok{ l }\AttributeTok{{-}lname} \StringTok{"*}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{/*"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{|} \ControlFlowTok{while} \BuiltInTok{read} \AttributeTok{{-}r} \VariableTok{broken\_link}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"Eliminando: }\VariableTok{$broken\_link}\StringTok{"}
    \FunctionTok{rm} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$broken\_link}\StringTok{"}
\ControlFlowTok{done}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✓ Limpieza completa!"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Casos de Uso Prácticos}\label{casos-de-uso-pruxe1cticos}

\subsection{Caso 1: Crear Dotfiles desde Cero (Primera
Vez)}\label{caso-1-crear-dotfiles-desde-cero-primera-vez}

\textbf{Escenario:} Nunca has usado Stow, quieres empezar desde cero
organizando tus configuraciones.

\textbf{Objetivo:} Crear estructura de dotfiles, migrar configs
existentes, versionar con Git.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Preparación}\label{paso-1-preparaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1.1 Instalar herramientas necesarias}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ stow git zsh starship  }\CommentTok{\# Arch/Archcraft}
\CommentTok{\# o}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt install stow git zsh        }\CommentTok{\# Kubuntu/Debian}

\CommentTok{\# 1.2 Verificar instalación}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}version}
\FunctionTok{git} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}version}

\CommentTok{\# 1.3 Crear directorio para dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 1.4 Inicializar Git}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ init}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ branch }\AttributeTok{{-}M}\NormalTok{ main}

\CommentTok{\# 1.5 Configurar Git (si no está configurado)}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ config user.name }\StringTok{"Edison Achalma"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ config user.email }\StringTok{"achalmaedison@gmail.com"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Crear Estructura de
Paquetes}\label{paso-2-crear-estructura-de-paquetes}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear paquetes para cada aplicación}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Git}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ git}
\CommentTok{\# Shell (Zsh)}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ shell}
\CommentTok{\# Terminal (Konsole)}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ terminal}
\CommentTok{\# Editor (VSCode)}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ vscode}
\CommentTok{\# KDE}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ kde}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Migrar Configuraciones
Existentes}\label{paso-3-migrar-configuraciones-existentes}

\textbf{3.1 Git (.gitconfig):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Verificar que existe}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig}

\CommentTok{\# Mover al paquete}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig git/}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ git/.gitconfig}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{3.2 Shell (Zsh):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear estructura}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ shell}

\CommentTok{\# Mover archivos}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc shell/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshenv shell/ }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}  \CommentTok{\# Si existe}

\CommentTok{\# Si tienes starship}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/starship.toml shell/ }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\ExtensionTok{tree}\NormalTok{ shell/}
\CommentTok{\# shell/}
\CommentTok{\# ├── .zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# └── .zshenv}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{3.3 Terminal (Konsole):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear estructura que replica HOME}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ terminal/.config}

\CommentTok{\# Mover config de Konsole}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/konsolerc terminal/.config/}

\CommentTok{\# Si tienes perfiles personalizados}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.local/share/konsole terminal/.local/share/ }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\ExtensionTok{tree}\NormalTok{ terminal/}
\CommentTok{\# terminal/}
\CommentTok{\# └── .config/}
\CommentTok{\#     └── konsolerc}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{3.4 VSCode:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear estructura}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ vscode/.config/Code/User}

\CommentTok{\# Mover settings}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code/User/settings.json vscode/.config/Code/User/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code/User/keybindings.json vscode/.config/Code/User/}

\CommentTok{\# Snippets}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code/User/snippets vscode/.config/Code/User/ }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\ExtensionTok{tree}\NormalTok{ vscode/.config/Code/User/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{3.5 KDE Plasma:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear estructura}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ kde/.config}

\CommentTok{\# Mover configuraciones principales}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/kdeglobals kde/.config/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/dolphinrc kde/.config/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/kwinrc kde/.config/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/plasmarc kde/.config/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/plasma{-}org.kde.plasma.desktop{-}appletsrc kde/.config/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/mimeapps.list kde/.config/}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\FunctionTok{ls}\NormalTok{ kde/.config/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Crear Ignore
Lists}\label{paso-4-crear-ignore-lists}

\textbf{4.1 Global ignore:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.stow{-}global{-}ignore }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Backups}
\StringTok{.*\textasciitilde{}}
\StringTok{.*\textbackslash{}.bak}
\StringTok{.*\textbackslash{}.old}
\StringTok{.*\textbackslash{}.orig}
\StringTok{.*\textbackslash{}.swp}

\StringTok{\# Historia}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.zsh\_history}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.bash\_history}

\StringTok{\# Cache}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.cache}
\StringTok{\_\_pycache\_\_}

\StringTok{\# Sistema}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.DS\_Store}
\StringTok{Thumbs\textbackslash{}.db}

\StringTok{\# Git}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.git}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.gitignore}

\StringTok{\# Documentación}
\StringTok{\^{}/README.*}
\StringTok{\^{}/LICENSE.*}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{4.2 Ignore por paquete (shell):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ shell/.stow{-}local{-}ignore }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Historia (datos sensibles)}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.zsh\_history}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.bash\_history}

\StringTok{\# Cache compilado}
\StringTok{\textbackslash{}.zcompdump}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{4.3 Ignore para VSCode:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ vscode/.stow{-}local{-}ignore }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Cache y logs}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/CachedData/}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/logs/}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/User/workspaceStorage/}

\StringTok{\# Backups automáticos}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/Backups/}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Crear .gitignore}\label{paso-5-crear-.gitignore}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ .gitignore }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# BACKUPS}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{*\textasciitilde{}}
\StringTok{*.bak}
\StringTok{*.old}
\StringTok{*.orig}
\StringTok{*.swp}
\StringTok{*.swo}

\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# DATOS SENSIBLES}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# Historia de shells}
\StringTok{**/.zsh\_history}
\StringTok{**/.bash\_history}

\StringTok{\# SSH keys}
\StringTok{**/.ssh/id\_*}
\StringTok{**/.ssh/*.pem}

\StringTok{\# Credenciales}
\StringTok{.netrc}
\StringTok{.authinfo}

\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# CACHE Y TEMPORALES}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{**/.cache/}
\StringTok{**/\_\_pycache\_\_/}
\StringTok{*.pyc}
\StringTok{.zcompdump*}

\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# STOW}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{.stow}

\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# LOGS}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{**/*.log}
\StringTok{*.log}

\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{\# SISTEMA}
\StringTok{\# ========================================}
\StringTok{.DS\_Store}
\StringTok{Thumbs.db}
\StringTok{desktop.ini}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Crear .stowrc}\label{paso-6-crear-.stowrc}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ .stowrc }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Target es siempre HOME}
\StringTok{{-}{-}target=$HOME}

\StringTok{\# Ignorar archivos comunes}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}\^{}\textbackslash{}.git\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}\^{}README.*\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}\^{}LICENSE.*\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}\textbackslash{}.gitignore$\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}.*\textbackslash{}.swp$\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}.*\textasciitilde{}$\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}\^{}install\textbackslash{}.sh$\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-}ignore=\textquotesingle{}\^{}\textbackslash{}.stowrc$\textquotesingle{}}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 7: Instalar con Stow (Primera
Vez)}\label{paso-7-instalar-con-stow-primera-vez}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Navegar a dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Dry run primero para cada paquete}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ git}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ shell}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ terminal}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ vscode}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ kde}

\CommentTok{\# Si todo OK, instalar realmente}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ git shell terminal vscode kde}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar symlinks}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx ... .gitconfig {-}\textgreater{} dotfiles/git/.gitconfig}

\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx ... .zshrc {-}\textgreater{} dotfiles/shell/.zshrc}

\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/konsolerc}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx ... konsolerc {-}\textgreater{} ../dotfiles/terminal/.config/konsolerc}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 8: Verificar que Todo
Funciona}\label{paso-8-verificar-que-todo-funciona}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 8.1 Verificar Git}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ config }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}list} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{head} \AttributeTok{{-}5}

\CommentTok{\# 8.2 Verificar Zsh}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{head} \AttributeTok{{-}10}

\CommentTok{\# 8.3 Verificar VSCode}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code/User/settings.json }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{head} \AttributeTok{{-}10}

\CommentTok{\# 8.4 Abrir aplicaciones para probar}
\ExtensionTok{code}  \CommentTok{\# VSCode debe cargar tu config}
\ExtensionTok{konsole}  \CommentTok{\# Konsole debe tener tu configuración}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 9: Crear Scripts de
Ayuda}\label{paso-9-crear-scripts-de-ayuda}

\textbf{9.1 Script de instalación:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ install.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\StringTok{set {-}e}

\StringTok{DOTFILES="$HOME/dotfiles"}

\StringTok{echo "🚀 Instalando dotfiles..."}

\StringTok{cd "$DOTFILES"}

\StringTok{\# Lista de paquetes}
\StringTok{PACKAGES=(}
\StringTok{    "git"}
\StringTok{    "shell"}
\StringTok{    "terminal"}
\StringTok{    "vscode"}
\StringTok{    "kde"}
\StringTok{)}

\StringTok{\# Instalar cada paquete}
\StringTok{for pkg in "$\{PACKAGES[@]\}"; do}
\StringTok{    echo "📦 Instalando $pkg..."}
\StringTok{    stow "$pkg"}
\StringTok{done}

\StringTok{echo "✅ ¡Instalación completa!"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x install.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{9.2 Script de verificación:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ check.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}

\StringTok{DOTFILES="$HOME/dotfiles"}

\StringTok{echo "📋 Verificando dotfiles..."}
\StringTok{echo ""}

\StringTok{cd "$DOTFILES"}

\StringTok{for pkg in */; do}
\StringTok{    pkg=$\{pkg\%/\}}
\StringTok{    }
\StringTok{    \# Buscar primer archivo}
\StringTok{    first\_file=$(find "$pkg" {-}type f | head {-}1)}
\StringTok{    }
\StringTok{    if [ {-}z "$first\_file" ]; then}
\StringTok{        continue}
\StringTok{    fi}
\StringTok{    }
\StringTok{    \# Path en HOME}
\StringTok{    home\_path="$HOME/$\{first\_file\#$pkg/\}"}
\StringTok{    }
\StringTok{    if [ {-}L "$home\_path" ]; then}
\StringTok{        echo "✅ $pkg"}
\StringTok{    else}
\StringTok{        echo "❌ $pkg (no instalado)"}
\StringTok{    fi}
\StringTok{done}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x check.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 10: Crear Repositorio en
GitHub}\label{paso-10-crear-repositorio-en-github}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 10.1 Agregar todo a Git}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add .}

\CommentTok{\# 10.2 Commit inicial}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Initial commit: Estructura básica de dotfiles}

\StringTok{{-} Git configuration}
\StringTok{{-} Zsh/Starship setup}
\StringTok{{-} Konsole terminal config}
\StringTok{{-} VSCode settings}
\StringTok{{-} KDE Plasma configuration"}

\CommentTok{\# 10.3 Crear repo en GitHub (vía navegador o gh CLI)}
\CommentTok{\# Opción A: Navegador}
\CommentTok{\# Ve a https://github.com/new}
\CommentTok{\# Nombre: .dotfiles}
\CommentTok{\# Descripción: "Dotfiles para Archcraft/Kubuntu con Stow"}
\CommentTok{\# Público o Privado}
\CommentTok{\# NO inicializar con README (ya lo tienes)}

\CommentTok{\# Opción B: GitHub CLI}
\ExtensionTok{gh}\NormalTok{ repo create .dotfiles }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}public} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}source}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{. }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}remote}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{origin}

\CommentTok{\# 10.4 Agregar remote y push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ remote add origin https://github.com/achalmaedison/.dotfiles.git}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push }\AttributeTok{{-}u}\NormalTok{ origin main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 11: Crear README.md}\label{paso-11-crear-readme.md}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ README.md }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Dotfiles}

\StringTok{Configuraciones personales para Archcraft/Kubuntu gestionadas con GNU Stow.}

\StringTok{\# Estructura}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textasciitilde/dotfiles/ ├── git/ \# Git config ├── shell/ \# Zsh +
Starship ├── terminal/ \# Konsole ├── vscode/ \# Visual Studio Code └──
kde/ \# KDE Plasma

\begin{verbatim}

# Instalación

```bash
# Clonar
git clone https://github.com/achalmaedison/.dotfiles.git ~/dotfiles

# Instalar Stow
sudo pacman -S stow  # Arch
# o
sudo apt install stow  # Debian/Ubuntu

# Instalar todo
cd ~/dotfiles
./install.sh

# O instalar selectivo
stow git shell terminal
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Actualizar}\label{actualizar}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Requisitos}\label{requisitos}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  stow
\item
  git
\item
  zsh
\item
  starship (opcional)
\item
  VSCode (opcional)
\item
  KDE Plasma (opcional)
\end{itemize}

\begin{verbatim}
EOF

git add README.md
git commit -m "docs: Add README"
git push
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Caso 2: Replicar Dotfiles en Laptop
Nueva}\label{caso-2-replicar-dotfiles-en-laptop-nueva}

\textbf{Escenario:} Acabas de comprar/instalar una laptop nueva con
Archcraft y quieres replicar tu setup completo.

\textbf{Objetivo:} Clonar tu repo de dotfiles e instalar todo en la
nueva máquina.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Preparar Nueva
Máquina}\label{paso-1-preparar-nueva-muxe1quina}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1.1 Actualizar sistema (Archcraft/Arch)}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}Syu}

\CommentTok{\# 1.2 Instalar herramientas base}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ git stow zsh base{-}devel}

\CommentTok{\# 1.3 Verificar HOME vacío (opcional)}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \StringTok{"\^{}\textbackslash{}."} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{wc} \AttributeTok{{-}l}
\CommentTok{\# Deberías ver solo archivos básicos del sistema}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Backup de Configs Existentes
(Precaución)}\label{paso-2-backup-de-configs-existentes-precauciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 2.1 Crear directorio de backup}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles{-}backup{-}}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +\%Y\%m\%d}\VariableTok{)}
\VariableTok{BACKUP\_DIR}\OperatorTok{=}\NormalTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles{-}backup{-}}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +\%Y\%m\%d}\VariableTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# 2.2 Backup de archivos que podrían existir}
\FunctionTok{cp}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc }\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}
\FunctionTok{cp}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig }\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code }\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"Backup guardado en: }\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Clonar
Repositorio}\label{paso-3-clonar-repositorio}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 3.1 Clonar tu repo}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/achalmaedison/.dotfiles.git dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 3.2 Verificar contenido}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}

\CommentTok{\# Deberías ver:}
\CommentTok{\# git/}
\CommentTok{\# shell/}
\CommentTok{\# terminal/}
\CommentTok{\# vscode/}
\CommentTok{\# kde/}
\CommentTok{\# install.sh}
\CommentTok{\# .gitignore}
\CommentTok{\# README.md}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Revisar y Ajustar (Si
Necesario)}\label{paso-4-revisar-y-ajustar-si-necesario}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 4.1 Ver qué paquetes hay}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}d} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\CommentTok{\# git/  kde/  shell/  terminal/  vscode/}

\CommentTok{\# 4.2 Ver estructura de un paquete}
\ExtensionTok{tree}\NormalTok{ shell/}
\CommentTok{\# shell/}
\CommentTok{\# ├── .zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# └── .zshenv}

\CommentTok{\# 4.3 (Opcional) Editar configs antes de instalar}
\CommentTok{\# Por ejemplo, cambiar username en git}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ git/.gitconfig}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Instalar
Dependencias}\label{paso-5-instalar-dependencias}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 5.1 Aplicaciones de tu setup}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S} \DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    zsh }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    starship }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    konsole }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    code }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{} } \CommentTok{\# VSCode (si está en repos)}
    \ExtensionTok{plasma{-}desktop} \DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    dolphin }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    kate }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    okular}

\CommentTok{\# 5.2 Si usas AUR (yay/paru)}
\CommentTok{\# VSCode desde AUR}
\ExtensionTok{yay} \AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ visual{-}studio{-}code{-}bin}

\CommentTok{\# Starship (si no está en repos oficiales)}
\ExtensionTok{yay} \AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ starship{-}bin}

\CommentTok{\# 5.3 Verificar instalaciones}
\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{ zsh}
\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{ starship}
\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{ code}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Dry Run
(Simulación)}\label{paso-6-dry-run-simulaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Navegar a dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Simular instalación para ver qué pasaría}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ git}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ shell}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ terminal}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ vscode}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ kde}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar que no hay errores}
\CommentTok{\# Si hay conflictos, verás warnings}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 7: Resolver Conflictos (Si
Existen)}\label{paso-7-resolver-conflictos-si-existen}

\textbf{Si ves algo como:}

\begin{verbatim}
WARNING! stowing shell would cause conflicts:
  * existing target is neither a link nor a directory: .zshrc
\end{verbatim}

\textbf{Resolver:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Opción A: Eliminar archivo existente}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# Opción B: Mover a backup (más seguro)}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles{-}backup{-}}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +\%Y\%m\%d}\VariableTok{)}\NormalTok{/}

\CommentTok{\# Luego intentar stow nuevamente}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ shell}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 8: Instalar Todo}\label{paso-8-instalar-todo}

\textbf{Opción A: Con script (recomendado):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{./install.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Opción B: Manual:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar uno por uno}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ git}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ Git instalado"}

\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ shell}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ Shell instalado"}

\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ terminal}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ Terminal instalado"}

\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ vscode}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ VSCode instalado"}

\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ kde}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✅ KDE instalado"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Opción C: Todo de una vez:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ git shell terminal vscode kde}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 9: Verificar
Instalación}\label{paso-9-verificar-instalaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 9.1 Verificar symlinks creados}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx ... .gitconfig {-}\textgreater{} dotfiles/git/.gitconfig}

\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx ... .zshrc {-}\textgreater{} dotfiles/shell/.zshrc}

\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code/User/settings.json}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx ... settings.json {-}\textgreater{} ../../../../dotfiles/vscode/.config/Code/User/settings.json}

\CommentTok{\# 9.2 Usar script de verificación}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{./check.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 10: Configurar
Shell}\label{paso-10-configurar-shell}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 10.1 Cambiar shell a Zsh (si no lo es)}
\FunctionTok{chsh} \AttributeTok{{-}s}\NormalTok{ /bin/zsh}

\CommentTok{\# 10.2 Logout y login para aplicar}
\CommentTok{\# O simplemente:}
\BuiltInTok{exec}\NormalTok{ zsh}

\CommentTok{\# 10.3 Verificar que Zsh cargó tu config}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \VariableTok{$SHELL}
\CommentTok{\# /bin/zsh}

\CommentTok{\# Ver prompt (si usas starship)}
\ExtensionTok{starship} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}version}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 11: Instalar Dependencias
Específicas}\label{paso-11-instalar-dependencias-especuxedficas}

\textbf{11.1 Extensiones de VSCode:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Si guardaste lista de extensiones}
\CommentTok{\# (Opción: guardar en dotfiles)}
\ExtensionTok{code} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}list{-}extensions} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/vscode/extensions.txt}

\CommentTok{\# En nueva máquina:}
\ControlFlowTok{while} \BuiltInTok{read} \AttributeTok{{-}r} \VariableTok{ext}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \ExtensionTok{code} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}install{-}extension} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$ext}\StringTok{"}
\ControlFlowTok{done} \OperatorTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/vscode/extensions.txt}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{11.2 Plugins de Zsh (si usas):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Oh{-}My{-}Zsh}
\FunctionTok{sh} \AttributeTok{{-}c} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$(}\ExtensionTok{curl} \AttributeTok{{-}fsSL}\NormalTok{ https://raw.github.com/ohmyzsh/ohmyzsh/master/tools/install.sh}\VariableTok{)}\StringTok{"}

\CommentTok{\# Zsh plugins (ejemplo: zsh{-}autosuggestions)}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/zsh{-}users/zsh{-}autosuggestions }\DataTypeTok{\textbackslash{}}
\NormalTok{    \textasciitilde{}/.oh{-}my{-}zsh/custom/plugins/zsh{-}autosuggestions}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{11.3 Temas de KDE (si los tienes):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Si tienes temas personalizados en dotfiles}
\CommentTok{\# Instalarlos desde System Settings}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 12: Probar Todo}\label{paso-12-probar-todo}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 12.1 Git}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ config }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}list} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ user}
\CommentTok{\# user.name=Edison Achalma}
\CommentTok{\# user.email=achalmaedison@gmail.com}

\CommentTok{\# 12.2 Zsh}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{head} \AttributeTok{{-}5}

\CommentTok{\# 12.3 VSCode}
\ExtensionTok{code}
\CommentTok{\# Debería cargar con tu configuración}

\CommentTok{\# 12.4 Konsole}
\ExtensionTok{konsole}
\CommentTok{\# Debería usar tu configuración}

\CommentTok{\# 12.5 KDE}
\CommentTok{\# Logout/login para ver cambios en KDE}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 13: Ajustes Finales}\label{paso-13-ajustes-finales}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Si algo no funciona, hacer debug:}

\CommentTok{\# Ver qué apunta cada symlink}
\FunctionTok{find}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{} }\AttributeTok{{-}maxdepth}\NormalTok{ 2 }\AttributeTok{{-}type}\NormalTok{ l }\AttributeTok{{-}ls} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Si un symlink está roto:}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ paquete{-}con{-}problema}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ paquete{-}con{-}problema}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar logs}
\ExtensionTok{journalctl} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}user} \AttributeTok{{-}xe} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \AttributeTok{{-}i}\NormalTok{ error}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 3: Actualizar Configs y
Sincronizar}\label{caso-3-actualizar-configs-y-sincronizar}

\textbf{Escenario:} Has estado usando tus dotfiles y has hecho cambios
en tu máquina principal. Quieres sincronizar con GitHub y otras
máquinas.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Identificar
Cambios}\label{paso-1-identificar-cambios}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1.1 Ver qué archivos cambiaron}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ status}

\CommentTok{\# Ejemplo de output:}
\CommentTok{\# modified:   shell/.zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# modified:   vscode/.config/Code/User/settings.json}

\CommentTok{\# 1.2 Ver diferencias específicas}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ diff shell/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ diff vscode/.config/Code/User/settings.json}

\CommentTok{\# 1.3 Ver todos los cambios}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ diff}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Probar Cambios
Localmente}\label{paso-2-probar-cambios-localmente}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Si editaste configs directamente en HOME (a través de symlinks),}
\CommentTok{\# los cambios ya están en \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}

\CommentTok{\# 2.1 Verificar que todo funciona}
\BuiltInTok{source}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# Para shell}
\ExtensionTok{code}  \CommentTok{\# Abrir VSCode para verificar settings}

\CommentTok{\# 2.2 Si hay problemas, revertir temporalmente}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}}\NormalTok{ shell/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# Revertir cambios}
\CommentTok{\# Probar de nuevo}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Commit Cambios}\label{paso-3-commit-cambios}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 3.1 Agregar archivos modificados}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add shell/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add vscode/.config/Code/User/settings.json}

\CommentTok{\# O agregar todo:}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add }\AttributeTok{{-}A}

\CommentTok{\# 3.2 Ver qué se va a commitear}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ status}

\CommentTok{\# 3.3 Commit con mensaje descriptivo}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"chore(shell): Update Zsh aliases and PATH}

\StringTok{{-} Add alias for git status}
\StringTok{{-} Update PATH to include \textasciitilde{}/.local/bin}
\StringTok{{-} Remove deprecated exports"}

\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"feat(vscode): Enable format on save}

\StringTok{{-} Set editor.formatOnSave to true}
\StringTok{{-} Add Python formatting rules}
\StringTok{{-} Update keybindings for terminal"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Push a GitHub}\label{paso-4-push-a-github}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 4.1 Push cambios}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}

\CommentTok{\# 4.2 Verificar en GitHub}
\CommentTok{\# Ir a https://github.com/achalmaedison/.dotfiles}
\CommentTok{\# Deberías ver tus commits recientes}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Actualizar Otras
Máquinas}\label{paso-5-actualizar-otras-muxe1quinas}

\textbf{En laptop/otra máquina:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 5.1 Pull cambios}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}

\CommentTok{\# 5.2 Los symlinks reflejan cambios automáticamente!}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# Ya tiene los cambios}

\CommentTok{\# 5.3 Recargar configs}
\BuiltInTok{source}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# Shell}
\CommentTok{\# VSCode se recarga automáticamente}

\CommentTok{\# 5.4 Si hay cambios en estructura (archivos nuevos/eliminados):}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ shell  }\CommentTok{\# Restow para actualizar symlinks}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ vscode}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Manejar Conflictos (Si
Existen)}\label{paso-6-manejar-conflictos-si-existen}

\textbf{Si modificaste el mismo archivo en dos máquinas:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}

\CommentTok{\# Si hay conflicto:}
\CommentTok{\# CONFLICT (content): Merge conflict in shell/.zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# 6.1 Ver conflicto}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ status}
\CommentTok{\# both modified:   shell/.zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# 6.2 Editar archivo}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ shell/.zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# Verás marcadores:}
\CommentTok{\# \textless{}\textless{}\textless{}\textless{}\textless{}\textless{}\textless{} HEAD}
\CommentTok{\# (tu cambio local)}
\CommentTok{\# =======}
\CommentTok{\# (cambio de GitHub)}
\CommentTok{\# \textgreater{}\textgreater{}\textgreater{}\textgreater{}\textgreater{}\textgreater{}\textgreater{} origin/main}

\CommentTok{\# 6.3 Resolver manualmente, eliminar marcadores}

\CommentTok{\# 6.4 Marcar como resuelto}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add shell/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"merge: Resolve conflict in .zshrc"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 4: Agregar Nueva Aplicación (Kate
Editor)}\label{caso-4-agregar-nueva-aplicaciuxf3n-kate-editor}

\textbf{Escenario:} Instalaste Kate y quieres agregar su configuración a
tus dotfiles.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Usar Kate y
Configurar}\label{paso-1-usar-kate-y-configurar}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1.1 Instalar Kate}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ kate}

\CommentTok{\# 1.2 Abrir y configurar}
\ExtensionTok{kate}

\CommentTok{\# Configurar:}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Settings → Configure Kate}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Cambiar tema, shortcuts, plugins, etc.}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Cerrar Kate (configs se guardan automáticamente)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Localizar Archivos de
Config}\label{paso-2-localizar-archivos-de-config}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 2.1 Archivos de configuración están en \textasciitilde{}/.config/}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ kate}
\CommentTok{\# drwxr{-}xr{-}x  {-} achalmaedison kate/}

\CommentTok{\# 2.2 Ver qué hay dentro}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/kate/}
\CommentTok{\# katerc}
\CommentTok{\# externaltools/}
\CommentTok{\# formatting/}
\CommentTok{\# lspclient/}

\CommentTok{\# 2.3 También puede haber datos en \textasciitilde{}/.local/share/}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.local/share/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ kate}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Crear Paquete
Kate}\label{paso-3-crear-paquete-kate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 3.1 Crear estructura que replica HOME}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ kate/.config}

\CommentTok{\# 3.2 Copiar configs (NO mover todavía)}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/kate kate/.config/}

\CommentTok{\# 3.3 También copiar datos locales si existen}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ kate/.local/share}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.local/share/kate kate/.local/share/ }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\CommentTok{\# 3.4 Verificar estructura}
\ExtensionTok{tree}\NormalTok{ kate/}
\CommentTok{\# kate/}
\CommentTok{\# ├── .config/}
\CommentTok{\# │   └── kate/}
\CommentTok{\# │       ├── katerc}
\CommentTok{\# │       ├── externaltools/}
\CommentTok{\# │       ├── formatting/}
\CommentTok{\# │       └── lspclient/}
\CommentTok{\# └── .local/}
\CommentTok{\#     └── share/}
\CommentTok{\#         └── kate/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Crear Ignore List para
Kate}\label{paso-4-crear-ignore-list-para-kate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear ignore para archivos que no queremos versionar}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ kate/.stow{-}local{-}ignore }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Sesiones y cache}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/kate/sessions/}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.local/share/kate/.*\textbackslash{}.cache}

\StringTok{\# Logs}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/kate/.*\textbackslash{}.log}

\StringTok{\# Archivos temporales}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/kate/.*\textbackslash{}.tmp}
\StringTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/kate/.*\textbackslash{}.swp}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Test Stow (Dry
Run)}\label{paso-5-test-stow-dry-run}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 5.1 Ver qué haría stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ kate}

\CommentTok{\# Deberías ver algo como:}
\CommentTok{\# LINK: .config/kate =\textgreater{} dotfiles/kate/.config/kate}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Hacer Backup y Eliminar
Original}\label{paso-6-hacer-backup-y-eliminar-original}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 6.1 Backup por seguridad}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/kate \textasciitilde{}/backup{-}kate{-}}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +\%Y\%m\%d}\VariableTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# 6.2 Eliminar original}
\FunctionTok{rm} \AttributeTok{{-}rf}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/kate}
\FunctionTok{rm} \AttributeTok{{-}rf}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.local/share/kate  }\CommentTok{\# Si copiaste esto también}

\CommentTok{\# 6.3 Verificar que se eliminó}
\FunctionTok{ls}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ kate}
\CommentTok{\# (no debería aparecer nada)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 7: Stow Kate}\label{paso-7-stow-kate}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 7.1 Instalar con stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ kate}

\CommentTok{\# 7.2 Verificar symlinks}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ kate}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx  {-} achalmaedison kate {-}\textgreater{} ../dotfiles/kate/.config/kate}

\CommentTok{\# 7.3 Verificar que Kate funciona}
\ExtensionTok{kate}
\CommentTok{\# Debería abrir con tu configuración}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 8: Versionar con
Git}\label{paso-8-versionar-con-git}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 8.1 Agregar al staging}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add kate/}

\CommentTok{\# 8.2 Commit}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"feat(kate): Add Kate editor configuration}

\StringTok{{-} Custom keybindings}
\StringTok{{-} LSP configuration}
\StringTok{{-} Theme and appearance settings}
\StringTok{{-} External tools setup"}

\CommentTok{\# 8.3 Push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 9: Actualizar
README}\label{paso-9-actualizar-readme}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar Kate a la lista de paquetes}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ README.md}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar:}
\CommentTok{\# {-} kate/          \# Kate editor}

\CommentTok{\# Commit cambio}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add README.md}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"docs: Add Kate to README"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 10: Actualizar Script de
Instalación}\label{paso-10-actualizar-script-de-instalaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Si tienes install.sh, agregar kate}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ install.sh}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar "kate" a la lista de PACKAGES:}
\CommentTok{\# PACKAGES=(}
\CommentTok{\#     "git"}
\CommentTok{\#     "shell"}
\CommentTok{\#     "terminal"}
\CommentTok{\#     "vscode"}
\CommentTok{\#     "kde"}
\CommentTok{\#     "kate"        \# \textless{}{-}{-} Agregar esto}
\CommentTok{\# )}

\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add install.sh}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"chore(scripts): Add kate to install script"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 5: Migrar de Kubuntu a
Archcraft}\label{caso-5-migrar-de-kubuntu-a-archcraft}

\textbf{Escenario:} Usabas Kubuntu, ahora instalaste Archcraft. Quieres
migrar tus dotfiles pero adaptándolos.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Evaluar
Diferencias}\label{paso-1-evaluar-diferencias}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1.1 En tu Kubuntu original, ver qué tienes}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}d} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}

\CommentTok{\# Ejemplo:}
\CommentTok{\# git/ shell/ terminal/ vscode/ kde/ digikam/ okular/ ...}

\CommentTok{\# 1.2 Identificar qué es compatible con Archcraft}
\CommentTok{\# ✅ Compatible: git, shell, vscode}
\CommentTok{\# ⚠️  Adaptar: kde (Archcraft puede usar i3/bspwm)}
\CommentTok{\# ❌ No necesario: apps específicas de Kubuntu}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: En Archcraft
Nueva}\label{paso-2-en-archcraft-nueva}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 2.1 Instalar Stow}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ stow git}

\CommentTok{\# 2.2 Clonar dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/achalmaedison/.dotfiles.git dotfiles}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Crear Branch para
Archcraft}\label{paso-3-crear-branch-para-archcraft}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 3.1 Crear branch específica}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout }\AttributeTok{{-}b}\NormalTok{ archcraft{-}setup}

\CommentTok{\# 3.2 Ver qué paquetes hay}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}d} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Instalar Paquetes
Universales}\label{paso-4-instalar-paquetes-universales}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 4.1 Paquetes que funcionan en cualquier distro}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ git}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ shell}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ terminal  }\CommentTok{\# Si Archcraft usa Konsole, sino adaptar}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Adaptar o Crear Nuevos
Paquetes}\label{paso-5-adaptar-o-crear-nuevos-paquetes}

\textbf{5.1 Window Manager (Si Archcraft usa i3/bspwm):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear nuevo paquete para i3 (ejemplo)}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ i3/.config/i3}

\CommentTok{\# Configurar i3}
\ExtensionTok{i3{-}config{-}wizard}
\CommentTok{\# O copiar config existente}

\CommentTok{\# Mover config al paquete}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/i3/config i3/.config/i3/}

\CommentTok{\# Stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ i3}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{5.2 Terminal (Si Archcraft usa otro terminal):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Supongamos que Archcraft usa Alacritty en vez de Konsole}

\CommentTok{\# Crear paquete}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ alacritty/.config/alacritty}

\CommentTok{\# Config de Alacritty}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ alacritty/.config/alacritty/alacritty.yml }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Alacritty configuration}
\StringTok{font:}
\StringTok{  size: 11.0}
\StringTok{  normal:}
\StringTok{    family: JetBrains Mono}
\StringTok{    }
\StringTok{window:}
\StringTok{  opacity: 0.95}
\StringTok{  }
\StringTok{colors:}
\StringTok{  \# Tu esquema de colores...}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# Stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ alacritty}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{5.3 Polybar (Si Archcraft lo usa):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ polybar/.config/polybar}

\CommentTok{\# Copiar config de Archcraft default}
\FunctionTok{cp}\NormalTok{ /etc/polybar/config polybar/.config/polybar/}

\CommentTok{\# Personalizar}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ polybar/.config/polybar/config}

\CommentTok{\# Stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ polybar}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: No Instalar Paquetes
Incompatibles}\label{paso-6-no-instalar-paquetes-incompatibles}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# NO hacer stow de paquetes específicos de Kubuntu/KDE:}
\CommentTok{\# {-} kde/}
\CommentTok{\# {-} plasma{-}org.kde.plasma.desktop{-}appletsrc}
\CommentTok{\# {-} etc.}

\CommentTok{\# Estos causarían errores en Archcraft}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 7: Commit Cambios}\label{paso-7-commit-cambios}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar nuevos paquetes}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add i3/ alacritty/ polybar/}

\CommentTok{\# Commit en branch archcraft}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"feat(archcraft): Add i3, Alacritty, Polybar configs}

\StringTok{{-} i3 window manager configuration}
\StringTok{{-} Alacritty terminal setup}
\StringTok{{-} Polybar panel configuration"}

\CommentTok{\# Push branch}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin archcraft{-}setup}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 8: Estrategia de
Branches}\label{paso-8-estrategia-de-branches}

\textbf{Opción A: Mantener branches separadas:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Branch main: Para Kubuntu}
\CommentTok{\# Branch archcraft{-}setup: Para Archcraft}

\CommentTok{\# Puedes hacer cherry{-}pick de commits específicos:}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ cherry{-}pick }\OperatorTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{commit{-}hash}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}  \CommentTok{\# Traer cambio específico de otra branch}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Opción B: Usar estructura de directorios:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Reorganizar dotfiles:}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ common/          }\CommentTok{\# Configs universales}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   ├── git/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   ├── shell/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   └── vscode/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ kubuntu/         }\CommentTok{\# Específicos de Kubuntu}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   ├── kde/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   └── konsole/}
\ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ archcraft/       }\CommentTok{\# Específicos de Archcraft}
    \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ i3/}
    \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ alacritty/}
    \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ polybar/}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar según distro:}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/common }\KeywordTok{\&\&} \ExtensionTok{stow} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/archcraft }\KeywordTok{\&\&} \ExtensionTok{stow} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 9: Script de Instalación por
Distro}\label{paso-9-script-de-instalaciuxf3n-por-distro}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ install{-}arch.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\StringTok{\# Install script para Archcraft}

\StringTok{DOTFILES="$HOME/dotfiles"}

\StringTok{echo "🚀 Instalando dotfiles para Archcraft..."}

\StringTok{\# Common packages}
\StringTok{cd "$DOTFILES/common"}
\StringTok{stow git shell vscode}

\StringTok{\# Archcraft{-}specific}
\StringTok{cd "$DOTFILES/archcraft"}
\StringTok{stow i3 alacritty polybar rofi}

\StringTok{echo "✅ ¡Instalación completa!"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x install{-}arch.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 10: Mantener Ambos
Sistemas}\label{paso-10-mantener-ambos-sistemas}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Cuando hagas cambios en configs comunes (git, shell, vscode):}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Hacer cambio en cualquier máquina}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ common/shell/.zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Commit}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add common/shell/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"chore(shell): Update aliases"}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}

\CommentTok{\# 4. En otra máquina (Kubuntu o Archcraft):}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}
\CommentTok{\# Los symlinks se actualizan automáticamente}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 6: Probar Nueva Configuración Sin
Romper}\label{caso-6-probar-nueva-configuraciuxf3n-sin-romper}

\textbf{Escenario:} Quieres probar una nueva configuración de Neovim sin
afectar tu setup actual.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Crear Branch
Experimental}\label{paso-1-crear-branch-experimental}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 1.1 Crear branch}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout }\AttributeTok{{-}b}\NormalTok{ experiment/nvim{-}lazyvim}

\CommentTok{\# 1.2 Verificar que estás en la branch}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ branch}
\CommentTok{\# * experiment/nvim{-}lazyvim}
\CommentTok{\#   main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Crear Paquete
Alternativo}\label{paso-2-crear-paquete-alternativo}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 2.1 Crear nuevo paquete con nombre distinto}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy/.config}

\CommentTok{\# 2.2 Instalar LazyVim (ejemplo)}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/LazyVim/starter nvim{-}lazy/.config/nvim}

\CommentTok{\# 2.3 Estructura}
\ExtensionTok{tree}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy/.config/nvim/ }\AttributeTok{{-}L}\NormalTok{ 1}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Desinstalar Neovim
Actual}\label{paso-3-desinstalar-neovim-actual}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 3.1 Unstow config actual (si existe)}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ nvim }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\CommentTok{\# 3.2 Verificar que se eliminó symlink}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{ nvim}
\CommentTok{\# No debería aparecer nada}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Instalar Nueva
Config}\label{paso-4-instalar-nueva-config}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 4.1 Stow nueva config}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy}

\CommentTok{\# 4.2 Verificar symlink}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim}
\CommentTok{\# lrwxrwxrwx  {-} achalmaedison nvim {-}\textgreater{} ../../dotfiles/nvim{-}lazy/.config/nvim}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Probar}\label{paso-5-probar}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 5.1 Abrir Neovim}
\ExtensionTok{nvim}

\CommentTok{\# LazyVim se instalará automáticamente}
\CommentTok{\# Probar todas las features}

\CommentTok{\# 5.2 Usar por varios días}
\CommentTok{\# Evaluar si te gusta}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Decidir Qué
Hacer}\label{paso-6-decidir-quuxe9-hacer}

\textbf{Opción A: Mantener nueva config (si te gustó):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Eliminar config vieja}
\FunctionTok{rm} \AttributeTok{{-}rf}\NormalTok{ nvim/  }\CommentTok{\# O hacer backup}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Renombrar nueva}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy nvim}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Restow}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ nvim}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Commit}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add .}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"refactor(nvim): Switch to LazyVim configuration"}

\CommentTok{\# 5. Merge a main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ merge experiment/nvim{-}lazyvim}

\CommentTok{\# 6. Push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}

\CommentTok{\# 7. Eliminar branch experimental}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ branch }\AttributeTok{{-}d}\NormalTok{ experiment/nvim{-}lazyvim}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Opción B: Volver a config anterior (si no te gustó):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Checkout a main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout main}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Unstow experimental}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Restow original}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ nvim}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Eliminar paquete experimental}
\FunctionTok{rm} \AttributeTok{{-}rf}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy/}

\CommentTok{\# 5. Eliminar branch}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ branch }\AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ experiment/nvim{-}lazyvim}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Opción C: Mantener ambas (para casos específicos):}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Tener dos configs de Neovim:}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ nvim/          }\CommentTok{\# Config principal}
\ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy/     }\CommentTok{\# Config alternativa}

\CommentTok{\# Alias en shell para cambiar:}
\BuiltInTok{alias}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}main=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}stow {-}D nvim{-}lazy \&\& stow nvim \&\& nvim\textquotesingle{}}
\BuiltInTok{alias}\NormalTok{ nvim{-}lazy=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}stow {-}D nvim \&\& stow nvim{-}lazy \&\& nvim\textquotesingle{}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 7: Sincronizar Múltiples Máquinas en Tiempo
Real}\label{caso-7-sincronizar-muxfaltiples-muxe1quinas-en-tiempo-real}

\textbf{Escenario:} Trabajas en 3 máquinas (desktop, laptop, servidor) y
quieres mantener dotfiles sincronizados.

\subsubsection{Configuración Inicial (Una
Vez)}\label{configuraciuxf3n-inicial-una-vez}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# En cada máquina:}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Clonar dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/achalmaedison/.dotfiles.git dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Instalar}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{./install.sh}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Configurar Git con pull automático (opcional)}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ config pull.rebase true  }\CommentTok{\# Rebase en lugar de merge}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Workflow Diario}\label{workflow-diario-1}

\textbf{Máquina A (Desktop) - Hacer Cambios:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1. Editar configs normalmente}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# Edita a través del symlink}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Commit y push}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add shell/.zshrc}
