\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"chore(shell): Add new alias for docker"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Máquina B (Laptop) - Recibir Cambios:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1. Pull cambios}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Los symlinks se actualizan automáticamente!}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# Ya tiene el cambio}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Recargar shell}
\BuiltInTok{source}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# O}
\BuiltInTok{exec}\NormalTok{ zsh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Máquina C (Servidor) - Recibir Cambios:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Mismo proceso}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}
\BuiltInTok{source}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Automatizar con Cron
(Opcional)}\label{automatizar-con-cron-opcional}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear script de sync}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/sync.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\StringTok{cd "$HOME/dotfiles"}

\StringTok{\# Pull cambios silenciosamente}
\StringTok{git pull origin main {-}{-}quiet}

\StringTok{\# Log}
\StringTok{echo "$(date): Dotfiles sincronizados" \textgreater{}\textgreater{} \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/sync.log}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/sync.sh}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar a crontab (sync cada hora)}
\FunctionTok{crontab} \AttributeTok{{-}e}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar línea:}
\ExtensionTok{0} \PreprocessorTok{*} \PreprocessorTok{*} \PreprocessorTok{*} \PreprocessorTok{*} \VariableTok{$HOME}\NormalTok{/dotfiles/sync.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Manejar Conflictos
Automáticamente}\label{manejar-conflictos-automuxe1ticamente}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Script más robusto}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/sync.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\StringTok{cd "$HOME/dotfiles"}

\StringTok{\# Stash cambios locales si existen}
\StringTok{git stash}

\StringTok{\# Pull}
\StringTok{git pull origin main {-}{-}quiet}

\StringTok{\# Reapply stash}
\StringTok{git stash pop}

\StringTok{\# Si hay conflictos, notificar}
\StringTok{if [ $? {-}ne 0 ]; then}
\StringTok{    notify{-}send "Dotfiles" "Conflicto detectado, revisar manualmente"}
\StringTok{fi}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Usar Git Hooks (Avanzado)}\label{usar-git-hooks-avanzado}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Pre{-}commit hook para validar antes de commit}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/.git/hooks/pre{-}commit }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}

\StringTok{\# Verificar que no hay datos sensibles}
\StringTok{if git diff {-}{-}cached | grep {-}i "password\textbackslash{}|secret\textbackslash{}|token"; then}
\StringTok{    echo "ERROR: Posible dato sensible detectado!"}
\StringTok{    exit 1}
\StringTok{fi}

\StringTok{exit 0}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/.git/hooks/pre{-}commit}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 8: Compartir Dotfiles con
Equipo/Lab}\label{caso-8-compartir-dotfiles-con-equipolab}

\textbf{Escenario:} Trabajas en un lab con múltiples usuarios y quieren
compartir configuraciones base.

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Crear Repo de
Equipo}\label{paso-1-crear-repo-de-equipo}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# En GitHub, crear repo:}
\CommentTok{\# Nombre: lab{-}dotfiles}
\CommentTok{\# Acceso: Privado/Público según necesidad}

\CommentTok{\# Clonar}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/lab/.lab{-}dotfiles.git \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Estructura
Multi-Usuario}\label{paso-2-estructura-multi-usuario}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Crear estructura}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p} \DataTypeTok{\{common}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{users\}}

\CommentTok{\# Common: Configs compartidas}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ common/}\DataTypeTok{\{git}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{shell}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{terminal\}}

\CommentTok{\# Users: Configs personales}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ users/}\DataTypeTok{\{alice}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{bob}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{carlos\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Setup Común}\label{paso-3-setup-comuxfan}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Git config compartido (sin user.name/email)}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ common/git/.gitconfig }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{[core]}
\StringTok{    editor = nano}
\StringTok{    autocrlf = input}

\StringTok{[alias]}
\StringTok{    st = status}
\StringTok{    co = checkout}
\StringTok{    br = branch}
\StringTok{    }
\StringTok{[push]}
\StringTok{    default = simple}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# Shell común}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ common/shell/.zshrc }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Shared Zsh configuration for Lab}

\StringTok{\# Common aliases}
\StringTok{alias ll=\textquotesingle{}ls {-}lah\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{alias ..=\textquotesingle{}cd ..\textquotesingle{}}

\StringTok{\# Lab{-}specific paths}
\StringTok{export LAB\_DATA="/data/lab"}
\StringTok{export LAB\_TOOLS="/opt/lab{-}tools"}

\StringTok{\# Source user{-}specific config if exists}
\StringTok{[ {-}f \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc.local ] \&\& source \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc.local}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Configs
Personales}\label{paso-4-configs-personales}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Usuario Alice}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ users/alice/.zshrc.local }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Alice\textquotesingle{}s personal config}

\StringTok{export EDITOR=nvim}

\StringTok{alias mydata=\textquotesingle{}cd /data/lab/alice\textquotesingle{}}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# Usuario Bob}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ users/bob/.zshrc.local }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Bob\textquotesingle{}s personal config}

\StringTok{export EDITOR=vim}

\StringTok{alias mydata=\textquotesingle{}cd /data/lab/bob\textquotesingle{}}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Script de
Instalación}\label{paso-5-script-de-instalaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ install{-}lab.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}

\StringTok{USERNAME="$1"}

\StringTok{if [ {-}z "$USERNAME" ]; then}
\StringTok{    echo "Uso: $0 \textless{}username\textgreater{}"}
\StringTok{    echo "Ejemplo: $0 alice"}
\StringTok{    exit 1}
\StringTok{fi}

\StringTok{DOTFILES="$HOME/lab{-}dotfiles"}

\StringTok{\# Instalar común}
\StringTok{cd "$DOTFILES/common"}
\StringTok{stow git shell terminal}

\StringTok{\# Instalar personal del usuario}
\StringTok{if [ {-}d "$DOTFILES/users/$USERNAME" ]; then}
\StringTok{    cd "$DOTFILES/users/$USERNAME"}
\StringTok{    stow .}
\StringTok{    echo "✅ Configs de $USERNAME instaladas"}
\StringTok{else}
\StringTok{    echo "⚠️  No hay configs personales para $USERNAME"}
\StringTok{fi}

\StringTok{echo "✅ Instalación completa para $USERNAME"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x install{-}lab.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Cada Usuario
Instala}\label{paso-6-cada-usuario-instala}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Usuario Alice:}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{./install{-}lab.sh}\NormalTok{ alice}

\CommentTok{\# Usuario Bob:}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{./install{-}lab.sh}\NormalTok{ bob}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 7: Actualizar Configs
Compartidas}\label{paso-7-actualizar-configs-compartidas}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Cualquier usuario puede actualizar common/}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Modificar}
\FunctionTok{nano}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles/common/shell/.zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Commit}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add common/shell/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"feat(shell): Add lab{-}wide utility function"}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}

\CommentTok{\# 4. Otros usuarios pull}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}
\CommentTok{\# Cambios se aplican automáticamente via symlinks}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 8: Usuarios Agregan Sus
Configs}\label{paso-8-usuarios-agregan-sus-configs}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Bob quiere agregar su config de Neovim}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Crear su directorio personal}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles/users/bob/.config}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Copiar config}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles/users/bob/.config/}

\CommentTok{\# 3. Commit (solo su carpeta)}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/lab{-}dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add users/bob/.config/nvim}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"feat(bob): Add Neovim configuration"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push}

\CommentTok{\# Otros usuarios no se afectan}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 9: Migrar de Sistema Manual a
Stow}\label{caso-9-migrar-de-sistema-manual-a-stow}

\textbf{Escenario:} Tienes dotfiles en GitHub pero SIN Stow (todos en
raíz del repo). Quieres migrar a Stow.

\subsubsection{Estado Inicial}\label{estado-inicial}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Tu repo actual (sin Stow):}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ .gitconfig}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ .zshrc}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ .zshenv}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ nvim/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   └── init.lua}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ konsolerc}
\ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ settings.json}

\CommentTok{\# Estructura plana, difícil de gestionar}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Backup Completo}\label{paso-1-backup-completo}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 1. Backup de dotfiles actuales}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles{-}backup{-}}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +\%Y\%m\%d}\VariableTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Backup de HOME}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/home{-}backup}
\FunctionTok{cp}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc \textasciitilde{}/home{-}backup/}
\FunctionTok{cp}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig \textasciitilde{}/home{-}backup/}
\CommentTok{\# etc...}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Crear Nueva
Estructura}\label{paso-2-crear-nueva-estructura}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Crear directorios de paquetes}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ git shell nvim terminal vscode}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Reorganizar
Archivos}\label{paso-3-reorganizar-archivos}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Git}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ .gitconfig git/}

\CommentTok{\# Shell}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ .zshrc shell/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ .zshenv shell/}

\CommentTok{\# Neovim (crear estructura correcta)}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ nvim/.config}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ nvim/ nvim/.config/nvim/}

\CommentTok{\# Terminal}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ terminal/.config}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ konsolerc terminal/.config/}

\CommentTok{\# VSCode}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ vscode/.config/Code/User}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ settings.json vscode/.config/Code/User/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Verificar Nueva
Estructura}\label{paso-4-verificar-nueva-estructura}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Debería verse así:}
\ExtensionTok{tree} \AttributeTok{{-}L}\NormalTok{ 3 \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}

\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\CommentTok{\# ├── git/}
\CommentTok{\# │   └── .gitconfig}
\CommentTok{\# ├── shell/}
\CommentTok{\# │   ├── .zshrc}
\CommentTok{\# │   └── .zshenv}
\CommentTok{\# ├── nvim/}
\CommentTok{\# │   └── .config/}
\CommentTok{\# │       └── nvim/}
\CommentTok{\# ├── terminal/}
\CommentTok{\# │   └── .config/}
\CommentTok{\# │       └── konsolerc}
\CommentTok{\# └── vscode/}
\CommentTok{\#     └── .config/}
\CommentTok{\#         └── Code/}
\CommentTok{\#             └── User/}
\CommentTok{\#                 └── settings.json}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Eliminar Symlinks/Archivos Viejos de
HOME}\label{paso-5-eliminar-symlinksarchivos-viejos-de-home}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Eliminar configs de HOME (los vamos a recrear con Stow)}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshenv}
\FunctionTok{rm} \AttributeTok{{-}rf}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/konsolerc}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/Code/User/settings.json}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Instalar con
Stow}\label{paso-6-instalar-con-stow}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Dry run primero}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ git shell nvim terminal vscode}

\CommentTok{\# Si todo OK, instalar}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ git shell nvim terminal vscode}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 7: Commit Nueva
Estructura}\label{paso-7-commit-nueva-estructura}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Stage todo}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add }\AttributeTok{{-}A}

\CommentTok{\# Ver cambios}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ status}

\CommentTok{\# Commit}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"refactor: Migrate to GNU Stow structure}

\StringTok{BREAKING CHANGE: Repository structure changed to use Stow}

\StringTok{{-} Organized configs into packages (git, shell, nvim, etc.)}
\StringTok{{-} Each package replicates HOME directory structure}
\StringTok{{-} Use \textquotesingle{}stow \textless{}package\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{} to install}

\StringTok{Migration guide:}
\StringTok{1. stow {-}D * (if already installed)}
\StringTok{2. stow git shell nvim terminal vscode"}

\CommentTok{\# Push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push origin main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 8: Actualizar
README}\label{paso-8-actualizar-readme}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ README.md }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Dotfiles (Stow{-}managed)}

\StringTok{Personal configurations managed with GNU Stow.}

\StringTok{\# Structure}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textasciitilde/dotfiles/ ├── git/ \# Git config ├── shell/ \# Zsh ├──
nvim/ \# Neovim ├── terminal/ \# Konsole └── vscode/ \# VSCode

\begin{verbatim}

# Installation

```bash
# Install Stow
sudo pacman -S stow

# Clone
git clone https://github.com/user/dotfiles.git ~/dotfiles

# Install all
cd ~/dotfiles
stow */

# Or selective
stow git shell nvim
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Update}\label{update}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
EOF

git add README.md
git commit -m "docs: Update README for Stow"
git push
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Paso 9: Crear Scripts}\label{paso-9-crear-scripts}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# install.sh}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ install.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\StringTok{cd "$HOME/dotfiles"}
\StringTok{stow git shell nvim terminal vscode}
\StringTok{echo "✅ Dotfiles installed"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x install.sh}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add install.sh}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"chore: Add install script"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 10: Limpiar Historial de Git
(Opcional)}\label{paso-10-limpiar-historial-de-git-opcional}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Si tu repo era muy grande con historia antigua,}
\CommentTok{\# puedes limpiarlo:}

\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Crear orphan branch}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}orphan}\NormalTok{ latest\_branch}

\CommentTok{\# Add all files}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add }\AttributeTok{{-}A}

\CommentTok{\# Commit}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"refactor: Fresh start with Stow structure"}

\CommentTok{\# Delete main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ branch }\AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ main}

\CommentTok{\# Rename current branch to main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ branch }\AttributeTok{{-}m}\NormalTok{ main}

\CommentTok{\# Force push}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push }\AttributeTok{{-}f}\NormalTok{ origin main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Caso 10: Setup para Desarrollo
Multi-Proyecto}\label{caso-10-setup-para-desarrollo-multi-proyecto}

\textbf{Escenario:} Trabajas en múltiples proyectos (Python, Web, Latex)
y quieres configs específicas por proyecto.

\subsubsection{Estructura de Dotfiles}\label{estructura-de-dotfiles}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ common/           }\CommentTok{\# Común a todo}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   ├── git/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   └── shell/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ python{-}dev/       }\CommentTok{\# Python development}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   ├── nvim/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   └── vscode/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ web{-}dev/          }\CommentTok{\# Web development}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   ├── nvim/}
\ExtensionTok{│}\NormalTok{   └── vscode/}
\ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ latex{-}writing/    }\CommentTok{\# Academic writing}
    \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ nvim/}
    \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ texstudio/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 1: Crear Estructura}\label{paso-1-crear-estructura}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Común}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ common/}\DataTypeTok{\{git}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{shell\}}

\CommentTok{\# Python dev}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ python{-}dev/}\DataTypeTok{\{nvim}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{vscode\}}

\CommentTok{\# Web dev}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ web{-}dev/}\DataTypeTok{\{nvim}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{vscode\}}

\CommentTok{\# LaTeX}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ latex{-}writing/}\DataTypeTok{\{nvim}\OperatorTok{,}\DataTypeTok{texstudio\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 2: Configs Comunes}\label{paso-2-configs-comunes}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Git (igual para todos)}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ common/git/.gitconfig }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{[user]}
\StringTok{    name = Edison Achalma}
\StringTok{    email = achalmaedison@gmail.com}

\StringTok{[core]}
\StringTok{    editor = nvim}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# Shell base}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ common/shell/.zshrc }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Common shell config}

\StringTok{\# Aliases}
\StringTok{alias gs=\textquotesingle{}git status\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{alias ll=\textquotesingle{}ls {-}lah\textquotesingle{}}

\StringTok{\# Load project{-}specific config}
\StringTok{[ {-}f \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc.project ] \&\& source \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc.project}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 3: Configs Específicas por
Proyecto}\label{paso-3-configs-especuxedficas-por-proyecto}

\textbf{Python Development:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Neovim para Python}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ python{-}dev/nvim/.config/nvim/init.lua }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-} Python{-}focused Neovim config}

\StringTok{{-}{-} LSP}
\StringTok{require(\textquotesingle{}lspconfig\textquotesingle{}).pyright.setup\{\}}

\StringTok{{-}{-} Python{-}specific keymaps}
\StringTok{vim.keymap.set(\textquotesingle{}n\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}\textless{}leader\textgreater{}r\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}:!python \%\textless{}CR\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{})}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# VSCode para Python}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ python{-}dev/vscode/.config/Code/User/settings.json }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\{}
\StringTok{    "python.linting.enabled": true,}
\StringTok{    "python.linting.pylintEnabled": true,}
\StringTok{    "python.formatting.provider": "black"}
\StringTok{\}}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# Shell additions para Python}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ python{-}dev/shell/.zshrc.project }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\# Python dev environment}

\StringTok{export PYTHONPATH="$HOME/projects/python:$PYTHONPATH"}

\StringTok{alias pytest=\textquotesingle{}python {-}m pytest\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{alias venv=\textquotesingle{}python {-}m venv venv \&\& source venv/bin/activate\textquotesingle{}}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Web Development:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Neovim para Web}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ web{-}dev/nvim/.config/nvim/init.lua }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{{-}{-} Web{-}focused Neovim config}

\StringTok{{-}{-} LSP for JS/TS}
\StringTok{require(\textquotesingle{}lspconfig\textquotesingle{}).tsserver.setup\{\}}

\StringTok{{-}{-} Live server}
\StringTok{vim.keymap.set(\textquotesingle{}n\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}\textless{}leader\textgreater{}l\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}:!live{-}server .\textless{}CR\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{})}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\CommentTok{\# VSCode para Web}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ web{-}dev/vscode/.config/Code/User/settings.json }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\{}
\StringTok{    "emmet.includeLanguages": \{}
\StringTok{        "javascript": "javascriptreact"}
\StringTok{    \},}
\StringTok{    "prettier.enable": true,}
\StringTok{    "editor.formatOnSave": true}
\StringTok{\}}
\OperatorTok{EOF}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 4: Scripts de
Activación}\label{paso-4-scripts-de-activaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Script para activar proyecto Python}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/activate{-}python.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}

\StringTok{echo "🐍 Activando entorno Python..."}

\StringTok{cd \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\StringTok{\# Unstow otros proyectos}
\StringTok{stow {-}D web{-}dev/nvim 2\textgreater{}/dev/null || true}
\StringTok{stow {-}D latex{-}writing/nvim 2\textgreater{}/dev/null || true}

\StringTok{\# Stow común}
\StringTok{stow common/*}

\StringTok{\# Stow Python}
\StringTok{stow python{-}dev/*}

\StringTok{\# Copiar project{-}specific shell config}
\StringTok{cp python{-}dev/shell/.zshrc.project \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc.project}

\StringTok{echo "✅ Entorno Python activado"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/activate{-}python.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Script para activar proyecto Web}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/activate{-}web.sh }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}

\StringTok{echo "🌐 Activando entorno Web..."}

\StringTok{cd \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\StringTok{\# Unstow otros}
\StringTok{stow {-}D python{-}dev/nvim 2\textgreater{}/dev/null || true}
\StringTok{stow {-}D latex{-}writing/nvim 2\textgreater{}/dev/null || true}

\StringTok{\# Stow común}
\StringTok{stow common/*}

\StringTok{\# Stow Web}
\StringTok{stow web{-}dev/*}

\StringTok{\# Shell config}
\StringTok{cp web{-}dev/shell/.zshrc.project \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc.project}

\StringTok{echo "✅ Entorno Web activado"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\FunctionTok{chmod}\NormalTok{ +x \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/activate{-}web.sh}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 5: Uso}\label{paso-5-uso}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Trabajar en proyecto Python}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/activate{-}python.sh}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/projects/python/my{-}project}
\ExtensionTok{nvim}  \CommentTok{\# Abre con config de Python}

\CommentTok{\# Cambiar a proyecto Web}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/activate{-}web.sh}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/projects/web/my{-}app}
\ExtensionTok{nvim}  \CommentTok{\# Abre con config de Web}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Paso 6: Automatizar con Direnv
(Avanzado)}\label{paso-6-automatizar-con-direnv-avanzado}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Instalar direnv}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ pacman }\AttributeTok{{-}S}\NormalTok{ direnv}

\CommentTok{\# En cada proyecto, crear .envrc}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/projects/python/my{-}project}
\FunctionTok{cat} \OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ .envrc }\OperatorTok{\textless{}\textless{} \textquotesingle{}EOF\textquotesingle{}}
\StringTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\StringTok{\# Activar entorno Python automáticamente}
\StringTok{source "$HOME/dotfiles/activate{-}python.sh"}
\OperatorTok{EOF}

\ExtensionTok{direnv}\NormalTok{ allow}

\CommentTok{\# Ahora al entrar al directorio, se activa automáticamente}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Implementación de Stow en mi
Repo}\label{implementaciuxf3n-de-stow-en-mi-repo}

\subsection{1. Script install.sh}\label{script-install.sh}

Mi \texttt{install.sh} actual debe usar Stow. Aquí está mi versión
mejorada:

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/install.sh}

\BuiltInTok{set} \AttributeTok{{-}e}

\VariableTok{DOTFILES}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles"}
\VariableTok{BACKUP\_DIR}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles{-}backup{-}}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{date}\NormalTok{ +\%Y\%m\%d{-}\%H\%M\%S}\VariableTok{)}\StringTok{"}

\CommentTok{\# Colores}
\VariableTok{RED}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textbackslash{}033[0;31m\textquotesingle{}}
\VariableTok{GREEN}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textbackslash{}033[0;32m\textquotesingle{}}
\VariableTok{YELLOW}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textbackslash{}033[1;33m\textquotesingle{}}
\VariableTok{NC}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textbackslash{}033[0m\textquotesingle{}} \CommentTok{\# No Color}

\CommentTok{\# Funciones}
\FunctionTok{log\_info()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \AttributeTok{{-}e} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{GREEN\}}\StringTok{[INFO]}\VariableTok{$\{NC\}}\StringTok{ }\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\FunctionTok{log\_warn()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \AttributeTok{{-}e} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{YELLOW\}}\StringTok{[WARN]}\VariableTok{$\{NC\}}\StringTok{ }\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\FunctionTok{log\_error()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \AttributeTok{{-}e} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{RED\}}\StringTok{[ERROR]}\VariableTok{$\{NC\}}\StringTok{ }\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar que Stow está instalado}
\ControlFlowTok{if} \OtherTok{! }\BuiltInTok{command} \AttributeTok{{-}v}\NormalTok{ stow }\OperatorTok{\&\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ /dev/null}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"Stow no está instalado"}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Instalando stow..."}
    \FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt update }\KeywordTok{\&\&} \FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt install }\AttributeTok{{-}y}\NormalTok{ stow}
\ControlFlowTok{fi}

\CommentTok{\# Función para hacer backup}
\FunctionTok{backup\_if\_exists()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{file}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}e} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$file}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]} \KeywordTok{\&\&} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{!} \OtherTok{{-}L} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$file}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"}
        \FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$file}\StringTok{"} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_warn} \StringTok{"Backup: }\VariableTok{$file}\StringTok{ {-}\textgreater{} }\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{/"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Función para stow paquete}
\FunctionTok{stow\_package()} \KeywordTok{\{}
    \BuiltInTok{local} \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"}
    
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Stowing }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{..."}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Dry run primero}
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}\&}\DecValTok{1} \KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \AttributeTok{{-}q} \StringTok{"WARNING"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_warn} \StringTok{"Conflicto detectado para }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \BuiltInTok{read} \AttributeTok{{-}p} \StringTok{"¿Hacer backup y continuar? [y/N] "} \AttributeTok{{-}n}\NormalTok{ 1 }\AttributeTok{{-}r}
        \BuiltInTok{echo}
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \KeywordTok{[[} \VariableTok{$REPLY} \OtherTok{=\textasciitilde{}} \PreprocessorTok{\^{}}\OperatorTok{[}\SpecialStringTok{Yy}\OperatorTok{]$} \KeywordTok{]];} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \CommentTok{\# Hacer backup de archivos conflictivos}
            \CommentTok{\# (aquí necesitarías lógica más sofisticada)}
            \ExtensionTok{stow} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{else}
            \ExtensionTok{log\_error} \StringTok{"Saltando }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
            \ControlFlowTok{return} \DecValTok{1}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{else}
        \ExtensionTok{stow} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"✓ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ instalado"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
\KeywordTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Cambiar a dotfiles directory}
\BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"} \KeywordTok{||} \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}

\CommentTok{\# Lista de paquetes a instalar}
\VariableTok{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{(}
    \StringTok{"git"}
    \StringTok{"shell"}
    \StringTok{"terminal"}
    \StringTok{"kde"}
    \StringTok{"vscode"}
    \StringTok{"nvim"}
    \StringTok{"kitty"}
    \CommentTok{\# ... más paquetes}
\VariableTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Opción para instalar todo o selectivo}
\ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{==} \StringTok{"all"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \VariableTok{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{($(}\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}d} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{sed} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}s\#/\#\textquotesingle{}}\VariableTok{))}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Instalando TODOS los paquetes"}
\ControlFlowTok{elif} \BuiltInTok{[} \VariableTok{$\#} \OtherTok{{-}gt}\NormalTok{ 0 }\BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \VariableTok{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{(}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$@}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{)}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Instalando paquetes especificados: }\VariableTok{$\{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{[*]}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}
\ControlFlowTok{fi}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar paquetes}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{[@]}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \ExtensionTok{stow\_package} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}
\ControlFlowTok{done}

\ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Instalación completa!"}
\ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}d} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \ExtensionTok{log\_info} \StringTok{"Backups guardados en: }\VariableTok{$BACKUP\_DIR}\StringTok{"}
\ControlFlowTok{fi}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Uso:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Instalar paquetes específicos}
\ExtensionTok{./install.sh}\NormalTok{ git shell terminal}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar todo}
\ExtensionTok{./install.sh}\NormalTok{ all}

\CommentTok{\# Ver qué haría sin hacer cambios}
\CommentTok{\# (modificar script para agregar {-}n flag)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{2. Script para Desinstalar}\label{script-para-desinstalar}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/uninstall.sh}

\VariableTok{DOTFILES}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles"}

\BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"} \KeywordTok{||} \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}

\ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \VariableTok{$\#} \OtherTok{{-}eq}\NormalTok{ 0 }\BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"Uso: }\VariableTok{$0}\StringTok{ \textless{}paquete1\textgreater{} [paquete2] ..."}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"O: }\VariableTok{$0}\StringTok{ all"}
    \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}
\ControlFlowTok{fi}

\ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$1}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{==} \StringTok{"all"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
    \VariableTok{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{($(}\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}d} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{sed} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}s\#/\#\textquotesingle{}}\VariableTok{))}
\ControlFlowTok{else}
    \VariableTok{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{(}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$@}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{)}
\ControlFlowTok{fi}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$\{PACKAGES}\OperatorTok{[@]}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"Unstowing }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{..."}
    \ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
    \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✓ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ desinstalado"}
\ControlFlowTok{done}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{3. Reorganizar Paquetes
Problemáticos}\label{reorganizar-paquetes-problemuxe1ticos}

\textbf{Zotero:} Ubicación no estándar

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Actual:}
\ExtensionTok{zotero/}
  \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ .zotero/zotero/25vfdnq5.default/}
      \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ prefs.js}

\CommentTok{\# Problema: .zotero está en HOME pero tiene subdirectorios profundos}

\CommentTok{\# Solución 1: Usar como está (funciona)}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ zotero}
\CommentTok{\# Resultado: \textasciitilde{}/.zotero/... → dotfiles/zotero/.zotero/...}

\CommentTok{\# Solución 2: Si solo quieres prefs.js, simplificar:}
\ExtensionTok{zotero/}
  \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ .zotero/}
      \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ zotero/}
          \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ 25vfdnq5.default/}
              \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ prefs.js}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Obsidian:} Ruta específica

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Actual:}
\ExtensionTok{obsidian/}
  \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ Documents/thoughts/.obsidian/}

\CommentTok{\# Problema: No está en .config sino en Documents}

\CommentTok{\# Solución: Está bien así, Stow lo maneja}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ obsidian}
\CommentTok{\# Resultado: \textasciitilde{}/Documents/thoughts/.obsidian → ...}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{4. .stowrc para tu Repo}\label{stowrc-para-tu-repo}

Crear \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/.stowrc}:

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/.stowrc}

\CommentTok{\# Target es siempre HOME}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}target=}\VariableTok{$HOME}

\CommentTok{\# Ignorar archivos comunes}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}.git\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}README.*\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}LICENSE.*\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}.*.swp\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}.*\textasciitilde{}\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}install.sh\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}uninstall.sh\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}ignore=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}.stowrc\textquotesingle{}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Con esto, no necesitas especificar \texttt{-t\ \textasciitilde{}} cada
vez.

\subsection{5. .stow-local-ignore por
Paquete}\label{stow-local-ignore-por-paquete}

\textbf{Para vscode:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/vscode/.stow{-}local{-}ignore}

\CommentTok{\# No stow extensiones (solo configuración)}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/CachedData/}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/logs/}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/Code/User/workspaceStorage/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Para kde:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/kde/.stow{-}local{-}ignore}

\CommentTok{\# Archivos de sesión y cache}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/session/}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.cache/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Para shell:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/shell/.stow{-}local{-}ignore}

\CommentTok{\# Historia de shells (puede tener info sensible)}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.zsh\_history}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.bash\_history}

\CommentTok{\# Archivos compilados}
\ExtensionTok{\textbackslash{}.zcompdump}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{6. Script de Verificación}\label{script-de-verificaciuxf3n}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#!/bin/bash}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/check{-}stow.sh}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar qué está stowed}

\VariableTok{DOTFILES}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/dotfiles"}

\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"Paquetes stowed:"}
\BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"================"}

\BuiltInTok{cd} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{"} \KeywordTok{||} \BuiltInTok{exit}\NormalTok{ 1}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ package }\KeywordTok{in} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{do}
    \VariableTok{package}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$\{package}\OperatorTok{\%}\NormalTok{/}\VariableTok{\}}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Encontrar primer archivo del paquete}
    \VariableTok{first\_file}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{find} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"} \AttributeTok{{-}type}\NormalTok{ f }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{head} \AttributeTok{{-}1}\VariableTok{)}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}z} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$first\_file}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \ControlFlowTok{continue}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Convertir a path en HOME}
    \VariableTok{home\_path}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$HOME}\StringTok{/}\VariableTok{$\{first\_file}\OperatorTok{\#}\VariableTok{$package}\NormalTok{/}\VariableTok{\}}\StringTok{"}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if} \BuiltInTok{[} \OtherTok{{-}L} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$home\_path}\StringTok{"} \BuiltInTok{]}\KeywordTok{;} \ControlFlowTok{then}
        \VariableTok{target}\OperatorTok{=}\VariableTok{$(}\FunctionTok{readlink} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$home\_path}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{)}
        \ControlFlowTok{if} \KeywordTok{[[} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$target}\StringTok{"} \OtherTok{==} \PreprocessorTok{*}\StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$DOTFILES}\StringTok{/}\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}\PreprocessorTok{*} \KeywordTok{]];} \ControlFlowTok{then}
            \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✓ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{"}
        \ControlFlowTok{else}
            \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✗ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ (symlink apunta a otro lugar)"}
        \ControlFlowTok{fi}
    \ControlFlowTok{else}
        \BuiltInTok{echo} \StringTok{"✗ }\VariableTok{$package}\StringTok{ (no stowed)"}
    \ControlFlowTok{fi}
\ControlFlowTok{done}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{7. Actualizar .gitignore}\label{actualizar-.gitignore}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/.gitignore}

\CommentTok{\# Backups}
\ExtensionTok{*\textasciitilde{}}
\ExtensionTok{*.bak}
\ExtensionTok{*.old}
\ExtensionTok{*.orig}
\BuiltInTok{.}\PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{.swp}

\CommentTok{\# Datos sensibles}
\ExtensionTok{shell/.zsh\_history}
\ExtensionTok{shell/.bash\_history}
\ExtensionTok{.netrc}
\ExtensionTok{.authinfo}

\CommentTok{\# Cache y temporales}
\ExtensionTok{**/.cache/}
\ExtensionTok{**/\_\_pycache\_\_/}
\ExtensionTok{**/node\_modules/}

\CommentTok{\# Logs}
\ExtensionTok{**/*.log}

\CommentTok{\# Sistema}
\ExtensionTok{.DS\_Store}
\ExtensionTok{Thumbs.db}

\CommentTok{\# Archivos de Stow}
\ExtensionTok{.stow}

\CommentTok{\# Zotero database (demasiado grande)}
\ExtensionTok{zotero/.zotero/zotero/*/zotero.sqlite*}

\CommentTok{\# VSCode workspace storage}
\ExtensionTok{vscode/.config/Code/User/workspaceStorage/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{8. Comandos Útiles para tu
Repo}\label{comandos-uxfatiles-para-tu-repo}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Navegar a dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar todo (primera vez)}
\ExtensionTok{./install.sh}\NormalTok{ all}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar paquetes esenciales}
\ExtensionTok{./install.sh}\NormalTok{ git shell terminal kde}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar qué está instalado}
\ExtensionTok{./check{-}stow.sh}

\CommentTok{\# Actualizar después de pull}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/  }\CommentTok{\# Restow todo}

\CommentTok{\# Desinstalar temporalmente para pruebas}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ vscode}
\CommentTok{\# hacer pruebas...}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ vscode  }\CommentTok{\# Reinstalar}

\CommentTok{\# Agregar nuevo paquete}
\FunctionTok{mkdir}\NormalTok{ new{-}app}
\CommentTok{\# crear estructura...}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ new{-}app}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add new{-}app/}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Add new{-}app"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Workflows Completos}\label{workflows-completos}

\subsection{Workflow 1: Configuración
Inicial}\label{workflow-1-configuraciuxf3n-inicial}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Paso 1: Crear estructura}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ init}

\CommentTok{\# Paso 2: Crear paquetes}
\FunctionTok{mkdir} \AttributeTok{{-}p}\NormalTok{ zsh nvim git}

\CommentTok{\# Paso 3: Mover configs existentes}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc zsh/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim nvim/.config/}
\FunctionTok{mv}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.gitconfig git/}

\CommentTok{\# Paso 4: Stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ zsh nvim git}

\CommentTok{\# Paso 5: Verificar}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc  }\CommentTok{\# debe ser symlink}

\CommentTok{\# Paso 6: Git}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add .}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Initial dotfiles"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ remote add origin git@github.com:user/dotfiles.git}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push }\AttributeTok{{-}u}\NormalTok{ origin main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Workflow 2: Día a Día}\label{workflow-2-duxeda-a-duxeda}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Editar configuración (desde cualquier lugar)}
\ExtensionTok{nvim}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim/init.lua  }\CommentTok{\# Edita a través del symlink}

\CommentTok{\# Commit cambios}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add nvim/}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Update nvim config: add new plugin"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ push}

\CommentTok{\# En otra máquina}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull}
\CommentTok{\# Los cambios se reflejan automáticamente (symlinks)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Workflow 3: Nueva
Máquina}\label{workflow-3-nueva-muxe1quina}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Clonar}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ clone https://github.com/user/dotfiles.git \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar Stow}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt install stow}

\CommentTok{\# Backup existentes (precaución)}
\FunctionTok{mkdir}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/backup}
\FunctionTok{cp}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc \textasciitilde{}/backup/ }\DecValTok{2}\OperatorTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{/dev/null }\KeywordTok{||} \FunctionTok{true}

\CommentTok{\# Stow}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar}
\FunctionTok{ls} \AttributeTok{{-}la}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/ }\KeywordTok{|} \FunctionTok{grep} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textbackslash{}{-}\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{}}

\CommentTok{\# Instalar dependencias de apps}
\CommentTok{\# (nvim plugins, zsh plugins, etc)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Workflow 4: Experimentar}\label{workflow-4-experimentar}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Crear branch de experimento}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout }\AttributeTok{{-}b}\NormalTok{ experiment{-}new{-}nvim}

\CommentTok{\# Modificar libremente}
\ExtensionTok{nvim}\NormalTok{ nvim/.config/nvim/init.lua}

\CommentTok{\# Restow para aplicar}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ nvim}

\CommentTok{\# Probar...}

\CommentTok{\# Si funciona:}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout main}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ merge experiment{-}new{-}nvim}

\CommentTok{\# Si no funciona:}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout main}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ nvim  }\CommentTok{\# Vuelve a main automáticamente}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Workflow 5: Actualización
Limpia}\label{workflow-5-actualizaciuxf3n-limpia}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Pull cambios}
\BuiltInTok{cd}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ pull origin main}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar qué cambió}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ log }\AttributeTok{{-}p} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}since}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{"1 week ago"}

\CommentTok{\# Desinstalar y reinstalar (limpia symlinks obsoletos)}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ nvim}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ nvim}

\CommentTok{\# O usar restow}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ nvim}

\CommentTok{\# Verificar que funciona}
\ExtensionTok{nvim} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}version}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Best Practices}\label{best-practices}

\subsection{1. Organización de
Paquetes}\label{organizaciuxf3n-de-paquetes}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Un paquete por aplicación}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ nvim/}
\ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ zsh/}
\ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ git/}

\CommentTok{\# Replicar estructura de HOME exactamente}
\ExtensionTok{nvim/}
  \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ .config/}
      \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ nvim/}
          \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ init.lua}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Múltiples aplicaciones en un paquete}
\ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/dotfiles/}
\ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ configs/}
    \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ .config/nvim/}
    \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ .config/kitty/}
    \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ .zshrc}

\CommentTok{\# Estructura diferente a HOME}
\ExtensionTok{nvim/}
  \ExtensionTok{└──}\NormalTok{ init.lua  }\CommentTok{\# ❌ Falta .config/nvim/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{2. Uso de Ignore Lists}\label{uso-de-ignore-lists}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Ignorar archivos sensibles}
\CommentTok{\# .stow{-}global{-}ignore}
\ExtensionTok{**/.history}
\ExtensionTok{**/.ssh/id\_*}
\ExtensionTok{.netrc}

\CommentTok{\# Ignorar cache por paquete}
\CommentTok{\# nvim/.stow{-}local{-}ignore}
\ExtensionTok{\^{}/\textbackslash{}.config/nvim/plugin/packer\_compiled\textbackslash{}.lua}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Commit archivos sensibles sin ignorar}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ add \textasciitilde{}/.ssh/id\_rsa  }\CommentTok{\# ❌ ¡NUNCA!}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{3. Commits y Mensajes}\label{commits-y-mensajes}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Commits descriptivos y atómicos}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"feat(nvim): Add LSP configuration for Rust"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"fix(zsh): Correct path to starship prompt"}

\CommentTok{\# Un cambio lógico por commit}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Commits genéricos}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Update stuff"}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Changes"}

\CommentTok{\# Múltiples cambios no relacionados en un commit}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{4. Testing Antes de Commit}\label{testing-antes-de-commit}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Siempre test antes de commit}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ nvim  }\CommentTok{\# Desinstalar}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ nvim     }\CommentTok{\# Reinstalar}
\CommentTok{\# Verificar que funciona}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ commit}

\CommentTok{\# Dry run en nueva máquina}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv} \PreprocessorTok{*}\NormalTok{/}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Commit sin probar}
\CommentTok{\# Cambios → commit → push → Rompe en otra máquina}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{5. Backup Siempre}\label{backup-siempre}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Backup antes de stow en nueva máquina}
\FunctionTok{mkdir}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/backup}
\FunctionTok{cp} \AttributeTok{{-}r}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim \textasciitilde{}/backup/}

\CommentTok{\# Luego stow}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ nvim}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Stow directamente sin backup}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ nvim  }\CommentTok{\# ❌ Puede sobrescribir configs importantes}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{6. Documentación}\label{documentaciuxf3n}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# README.md completo}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Qué paquetes hay}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Cómo instalar}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Dependencias}
\CommentTok{\# {-} Comandos útiles}

\CommentTok{\# Comentarios en configs}
\CommentTok{\# nvim/init.lua}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}}\NormalTok{ LSP configuration}
\ExtensionTok{{-}{-}}\NormalTok{ Requires: nvim{-}lspconfig plugin}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# README vacío o sin info}
\CommentTok{\# Configs sin comentarios}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{7. Estructura Consistente}\label{estructura-consistente}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Misma estructura en todos los paquetes}
\ExtensionTok{package/}
  \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ .stow{-}local{-}ignore}
  \ExtensionTok{├──}\NormalTok{ README.md}
  \ExtensionTok{└──} \ErrorTok{(}\ExtensionTok{archivos}\NormalTok{ que van en HOME}\KeywordTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Estructura inconsistente entre paquetes}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{8. Versionado}\label{versionado}

\textbf{DO:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Tags para versiones estables}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ tag }\AttributeTok{{-}a}\NormalTok{ v1.0.0 }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Stable nvim config"}

\CommentTok{\# Branches para experimentar}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ checkout }\AttributeTok{{-}b}\NormalTok{ experiment/new{-}theme}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{DON'T:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Todo en main sin tags}
\CommentTok{\# Experimentar directamente en main}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Alternativas a Stow}\label{alternativas-a-stow}

\subsection{1. yadm (Yet Another Dotfiles
Manager)}\label{yadm-yet-another-dotfiles-manager}

\textbf{Ventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Git nativo, no symlinks
\item
  Encriptación built-in
\item
  Templates con Jinja2
\item
  Bootstrap scripts
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Desventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Menos control granular
\item
  Todo en un repo
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Instalar}
\FunctionTok{sudo}\NormalTok{ apt install yadm}

\CommentTok{\# Usar}
\ExtensionTok{yadm}\NormalTok{ init}
\ExtensionTok{yadm}\NormalTok{ add \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\ExtensionTok{yadm}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Add zshrc"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{2. chezmoi}\label{chezmoi}

\textbf{Ventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Templates
\item
  Secrets management
\item
  Cross-platform
\item
  Estado vs archivos
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Desventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Más complejo
\item
  Curva de aprendizaje
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Instalar}
\FunctionTok{sh} \AttributeTok{{-}c} \StringTok{"}\VariableTok{$(}\ExtensionTok{curl} \AttributeTok{{-}fsLS}\NormalTok{ get.chezmoi.io}\VariableTok{)}\StringTok{"}

\CommentTok{\# Usar}
\ExtensionTok{chezmoi}\NormalTok{ init}
\ExtensionTok{chezmoi}\NormalTok{ add \textasciitilde{}/.zshrc}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{3. dotbot}\label{dotbot}

\textbf{Ventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Basado en configuración YAML
\item
  Bootstrapping automático
\item
  Plugins
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Desventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Otra herramienta que aprender
\item
  Menos flexibilidad que Stow
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# install.conf.yaml}
\ExtensionTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ link:}
    \ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/.zshrc:}\NormalTok{ zshrc}
    \ExtensionTok{\textasciitilde{}/.config/nvim:}\NormalTok{ nvim}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{4. Bare Git Repository}\label{bare-git-repository}

\textbf{Ventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Solo Git, no tools extra
\item
  Total control
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Desventajas:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Más manual
\item
  Conflictos con .gitignore
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Setup}
\FunctionTok{git}\NormalTok{ init }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}bare} \VariableTok{$HOME}\NormalTok{/.dotfiles}
\BuiltInTok{alias}\NormalTok{ config=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}/usr/bin/git {-}{-}git{-}dir=$HOME/.dotfiles/ {-}{-}work{-}tree=$HOME\textquotesingle{}}
\ExtensionTok{config}\NormalTok{ config }\AttributeTok{{-}{-}local}\NormalTok{ status.showUntrackedFiles no}

\CommentTok{\# Usar}
\ExtensionTok{config}\NormalTok{ add .zshrc}
\ExtensionTok{config}\NormalTok{ commit }\AttributeTok{{-}m} \StringTok{"Add zshrc"}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Comparación}\label{comparaciuxf3n}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2101}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1377}}

  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1884}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1377}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 10\tabcolsep) * \real{0.1884}}@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}

Característica
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
GNU Stow
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
yadm
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
chezmoi
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright

Repositorio Git bare
\end{minipage} \\

Simplicidad & Muy alta & Alta & Media & Alta & Media \\
Flexibilidad & Muy alta & Media & Muy alta & Media & Muy alta \\
Soporte para plantillas & No & Parcial & Completo (Jinja2) & No & No \\
Manejo de secretos & No & Bueno & Excelente (integrado) & No & No \\

Muy sencilla & Sin instalación adicional \\

(herramienta nativa) \\

No \\

\end{longtable}

\textbf{Recomendación:} Stow es ideal si quieres:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Simplicidad
\item
  Control total
\item
  Organización por paquetes
\item
  Solo symlinks, sin magia
\end{itemize}

\section{Conclusión}\label{conclusiuxf3n}

La gestión de dotfiles es una práctica esencial para optimizar el
entorno de desarrollo y asegurar la persistencia de las configuraciones
personalizadas. GNU Stow, en particular, se destaca por su simplicidad y
eficacia al manejar enlaces simbólicos, especialmente cuando se combina
con Git para el versionado y la sincronización. Permite una modularidad
excelente y una replicación rápida de entornos.

Si bien existen alternativas más avanzadas como Chezmoi o YADM (que
ofrecen funciones adicionales como plantillas y cifrado de secretos) o
soluciones declarativas como NixOS/Home-Manager, Stow sigue siendo una
opción robusta y preferida por muchos por su enfoque directo y la curva
de aprendizaje mínima. La clave es elegir la herramienta que mejor se
adapte a las necesidades y al nivel de complejidad deseado, siempre
priorizando la seguridad de la información sensible.

\subsection{Comandos Esenciales}\label{comandos-esenciales}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Instalar}
\ExtensionTok{stow}\NormalTok{ paquete}

\CommentTok{\# Desinstalar}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}D}\NormalTok{ paquete}

\CommentTok{\# Reinstalar}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}R}\NormalTok{ paquete}

\CommentTok{\# Simular}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}nv}\NormalTok{ paquete}

\CommentTok{\# Ver qué hace}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}vv}\NormalTok{ paquete}

\CommentTok{\# Ignorar archivos}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}{-}ignore}\OperatorTok{=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}patrón\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{ paquete}

\CommentTok{\# Especificar directorios}
\ExtensionTok{stow} \AttributeTok{{-}d}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{}/dotfiles }\AttributeTok{{-}t}\NormalTok{ \textasciitilde{} paquete}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}
